```
     1→import { getAllFlows, getFlowBySlug, getAdjacentFlows, buildSlugRegistry, getBacklinks } from '@/lib/markdown';
     2→import { siteConfig } from '../../../../../../site.config';
     3→import { Metadata } from 'next';
     4→import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
     5→import { t, resolveLocale } from '@/lib/i18n';
     6→import FlowCalendarSidebar from '@/components/FlowCalendarSidebar';
     7→import Tag from '@/components/Tag';
     8→import MarkdownRenderer from '@/components/MarkdownRenderer';
     9→import Backlinks from '@/components/Backlinks';
    10→import ShareBar from '@/components/ShareBar';
    11→import Link from 'next/link';
    12→
    13→export function generateStaticParams() {
    14→  if (siteConfig.features?.flow?.enabled === false) return [{ year: '_', month: '_', day: '_' }];
    15→  const allFlows = getAllFlows();
    16→  if (allFlows.length === 0) return [{ year: '_', month: '_', day: '_' }];
    17→  return allFlows.map(flow => {
    18→    const [year, month, day] = flow.slug.split('/');
    19→    return { year, month, day };
    20→  });
    21→}
    22→
    23→export const dynamicParams = false;
    24→
    25→export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ year: string; month: string; day: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
    26→  const { year, month, day } = await params;
    27→  const flow = getFlowBySlug(`${year}/${month}/${day}`);
    28→  if (!flow) return { title: 'Not Found' };
    29→  return {
    30→    title: `${flow.title} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    31→    description: flow.excerpt,
    32→    openGraph: {
    33→      title: flow.title,
    34→      description: flow.excerpt,
    35→      type: 'article',
    36→      publishedTime: flow.date,
    37→      url: `${siteConfig.baseUrl}/flows/${year}/${month}/${day}`,
    38→      siteName: resolveLocale(siteConfig.title),
    39→    },
    40→    twitter: {
    41→      card: 'summary',
    42→      title: flow.title,
    43→      description: flow.excerpt,
    44→    },
    45→  };
    46→}
    47→
    48→export default async function FlowPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ year: string; month: string; day: string }> }) {
    49→  if (siteConfig.features?.flow?.enabled === false) notFound();
    50→  const { year, month, day } = await params;
    51→  const slug = `${year}/${month}/${day}`;
    52→  const flow = getFlowBySlug(slug);
    53→  if (!flow) notFound();
    54→
    55→  const allFlows = getAllFlows();
    56→  const entryDates = allFlows.map(f => f.date);
    57→  const { prev, next } = getAdjacentFlows(flow.slug);
    58→  const slugRegistry = buildSlugRegistry();
    59→  const backlinks = getBacklinks(flow.slug);
    60→  const flowUrl = `${siteConfig.baseUrl}/flows/${year}/${month}/${day}`;
    61→
    62→  const breadcrumb = (
    63→    <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-muted">
    64→      <Link href="/flows" className="hover:text-accent no-underline shrink-0">
    65→        {t('all_flows')}
    66→      </Link>
    67→      <span className="text-muted/40" aria-hidden="true">›</span>
    68→      <Link href={`/flows/${year}`} className="hover:text-accent no-underline shrink-0">
    69→        {year}
    70→      </Link>
    71→      <span className="text-muted/40" aria-hidden="true">›</span>
    72→      <Link href={`/flows/${year}/${month}`} className="hover:text-accent no-underline shrink-0">
    73→        {month}
    74→      </Link>
    75→      <span className="text-muted/40" aria-hidden="true">›</span>
    76→      <span className="text-foreground shrink-0">{day}</span>
    77→    </nav>
    78→  );
    79→
    80→  return (
    81→    <div className="layout-main">
    82→      <div className="flex gap-10">
    83→        <FlowCalendarSidebar entryDates={entryDates} currentDate={flow.date} breadcrumb={breadcrumb} />
    84→
    85→        <article className="flex-1 min-w-0">
    86→          {/* Header */}
    87→          <header className="mb-8">
    88→            <time className="text-base font-mono text-accent" data-pagefind-meta="date[content]">{flow.date}</time>
    89→            {flow.tags.length > 0 && (
    90→              <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
    91→                {flow.tags.map(tag => (
    92→                  <Tag key={tag} tag={tag} variant="compact" />
    93→                ))}
    94→              </div>
    95→            )}
    96→          </header>
    97→
    98→          {/* Content */}
    99→          <div className="prose prose-lg dark:prose-invert max-w-none">
   100→            <MarkdownRenderer content={flow.content} slug={`flows/${year}/${month}/${day}`} slugRegistry={slugRegistry} />
   101→          </div>
   102→
   103→          <Backlinks backlinks={backlinks} />
   104→
   105→          <ShareBar url={flowUrl} title={flow.title} className="mt-8 mb-2" />
   106→
   107→          {/* Prev/Next navigation */}
   108→          <nav aria-label="Post navigation" className="mt-12 pt-12 border-t border-muted/20 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
   109→            {prev ? (
   110→              <Link
   111→                href={`/flows/${prev.slug}`}
   112→                className="group text-left no-underline"
   113→              >
   114→                <span className="text-xs text-muted">{t('older')}</span>
   115→                <div className="text-sm font-mono text-heading group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">
   116→                  {prev.date}
   117→                </div>
   118→              </Link>
   119→            ) : <div />}
   120→            {next ? (
   121→              <Link
   122→                href={`/flows/${next.slug}`}
   123→                className="group text-right no-underline"
   124→              >
   125→                <span className="text-xs text-muted">{t('newer')}</span>
   126→                <div className="text-sm font-mono text-heading group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">
   127→                  {next.date}
   128→                </div>
   129→              </Link>
   130→            ) : <div />}
   131→          </nav>
   132→        </article>
   133→      </div>
   134→    </div>
   135→  );
   136→}
```

```
     1→'use client'
     2→
     3→import { useState } from 'react';
     4→import { IconType } from 'react-icons';
     5→import { FaXTwitter, FaFacebook, FaLinkedin, FaWeibo, FaRedditAlien, FaTelegram, FaMastodon, FaHackerNews } from 'react-icons/fa6';
     6→import { SiBluesky, SiDouban, SiZhihu } from 'react-icons/si';
     7→import { LuLink, LuCheck } from 'react-icons/lu';
     8→import { siteConfig } from '../../site.config';
     9→import { useLanguage } from './LanguageProvider';
    10→
    11→interface ShareBarProps {
    12→  url: string;
    13→  title: string;
    14→  className?: string;
    15→}
    16→
    17→type Platform =
    18→  | 'twitter' | 'facebook' | 'linkedin' | 'weibo'
    19→  | 'reddit' | 'hackernews' | 'telegram' | 'bluesky' | 'mastodon'
    20→  | 'douban' | 'zhihu'
    21→  | 'copy';
    22→
    23→const PLATFORM_META: Record<Platform, { label: string; Icon: IconType }> = {
    24→  twitter:    { label: 'X / Twitter', Icon: FaXTwitter },
    25→  facebook:   { label: 'Facebook',    Icon: FaFacebook },
    26→  linkedin:   { label: 'LinkedIn',    Icon: FaLinkedin },
    27→  weibo:      { label: '微博',         Icon: FaWeibo },
    28→  reddit:     { label: 'Reddit',      Icon: FaRedditAlien },
    29→  hackernews: { label: 'Hacker News', Icon: FaHackerNews },
    30→  telegram:   { label: 'Telegram',    Icon: FaTelegram },
    31→  bluesky:    { label: 'Bluesky',     Icon: SiBluesky },
    32→  mastodon:   { label: 'Mastodon',    Icon: FaMastodon },
    33→  douban:     { label: '豆瓣',         Icon: SiDouban },
    34→  zhihu:      { label: '知乎',         Icon: SiZhihu },
    35→  copy:       { label: 'Copy link',   Icon: LuLink },
    36→};
    37→
    38→function getShareUrl(platform: Platform, url: string, title: string): string {
    39→  const eu = encodeURIComponent(url);
    40→  const et = encodeURIComponent(title);
    41→  const combined = encodeURIComponent(`${title} ${url}`);
    42→  switch (platform) {
    43→    case 'twitter':    return `https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?text=${et}&url=${eu}`;
    44→    case 'facebook':   return `https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=${eu}`;
    45→    case 'linkedin':   return `https://www.linkedin.com/sharing/share-offsite/?url=${eu}`;
    46→    case 'weibo':      return `https://service.weibo.com/share/share.php?url=${eu}&title=${et}`;
    47→    case 'reddit':     return `https://www.reddit.com/submit?url=${eu}&title=${et}`;
    48→    case 'hackernews': return `https://news.ycombinator.com/submitlink?u=${eu}&t=${et}`;
    49→    case 'telegram':   return `https://t.me/share/url?url=${eu}&text=${et}`;
    50→    case 'bluesky':    return `https://bsky.app/intent/compose?text=${combined}`;
    51→    case 'mastodon':   return `https://mastodon.social/share?text=${combined}`;
    52→    case 'douban':     return `https://www.douban.com/share/service?href=${eu}&name=${et}`;
    53→    case 'zhihu':      return `https://www.zhihu.com/share?href=${eu}&type=text&title=${et}`;
    54→    case 'copy':       return '';
    55→  }
    56→}
    57→
    58→export default function ShareBar({ url, title, className = '' }: ShareBarProps) {
    59→  const { t } = useLanguage();
    60→  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
    61→
    62→  if (!siteConfig.share?.enabled) return null;
    63→  const configured = siteConfig.share?.platforms ?? [];
    64→  const platforms = configured.filter((p): p is Platform => p in PLATFORM_META);
    65→  if (platforms.length === 0) return null;
    66→
    67→  const handleCopy = async () => {
    68→    try {
    69→      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
    70→      setCopied(true);
    71→      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    72→    } catch {
    73→      // clipboard not available
    74→    }
    75→  };
    76→
    77→  const btnClass = 'inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded text-muted hover:text-accent hover:bg-muted/10 transition-colors';
    78→
    79→  return (
    80→    <div className={`flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-1 ${className}`}>
    81→      {platforms.map((platform) => {
    82→        const { label, Icon } = PLATFORM_META[platform];
    83→
    84→        if (platform === 'copy') {
    85→          const copyLabel = copied ? t('link_copied') : t('copy_link');
    86→          return (
    87→            <button
    88→              key={platform}
    89→              onClick={handleCopy}
    90→              title={copyLabel}
    91→              aria-label={copyLabel}
    92→              className={`${btnClass} ${copied ? 'text-accent' : ''}`}
    93→            >
    94→              {copied ? <LuCheck size={16} /> : <Icon size={16} />}
    95→            </button>
    96→          );
    97→        }
    98→
    99→        return (
   100→          <a
   101→            key={platform}
   102→            href={getShareUrl(platform, url, title)}
   103→            target="_blank"
   104→            rel="noopener noreferrer"
   105→            title={label}
   106→            aria-label={`Share on ${label}`}
   107→            className={`${btnClass} no-underline`}
   108→          >
   109→            <Icon size={16} />
   110→          </a>
   111→        );
   112→      })}
   113→    </div>
   114→  );
   115→}
   116→
```

```
     1→import Link from 'next/link';
     2→
     3→interface TagProps {
     4→  tag: string;
     5→  count?: number;
     6→  variant?: 'default' | 'compact' | 'large';
     7→  showHash?: boolean;
     8→}
     9→
    10→/**
    11→ * Unified tag component for consistent styling across the site.
    12→ * Variants:
    13→ * - default: Standard pill-style tag (for post headers)
    14→ * - compact: Minimal inline style (for post lists)
    15→ * - large: Card-style with optional count (for tag cloud)
    16→ */
    17→export default function Tag({ tag, count, variant = 'default', showHash = true }: TagProps) {
    18→  const baseClasses = 'no-underline transition-colors duration-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent/50';
    19→
    20→  const variantClasses = {
    21→    default: 'inline-flex px-3 py-1 bg-muted/10 rounded-full text-xs font-medium text-muted hover:bg-accent/10 hover:text-accent',
    22→    compact: 'text-xs text-muted hover:text-accent',
    23→    large: 'group relative inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-muted/20 bg-muted/5 hover:bg-background hover:border-accent hover:shadow-md hover:shadow-accent/5',
    24→  };
    25→
    26→  return (
    27→    <Link
    28→      href={`/tags/${tag.toLowerCase()}`}
    29→      className={`${baseClasses} ${variantClasses[variant]}`}
    30→    >
    31→      {variant === 'large' ? (
    32→        <>
    33→          <span className="font-sans font-medium text-foreground group-hover:text-accent transition-colors text-sm">
    34→            {tag}
    35→          </span>
    36→          {count !== undefined && (
    37→            <span className="ml-2 text-xs font-mono text-muted/60 group-hover:text-accent/60">
    38→              {count}
    39→            </span>
    40→          )}
    41→        </>
    42→      ) : (
    43→        <span>{showHash ? '#' : ''}{tag}</span>
    44→      )}
    45→    </Link>
    46→  );
    47→}
    48→
```

```
     2→
     3→import Giscus from '@giscus/react';
     4→import { siteConfig } from '../../site.config';
     5→import { useTheme } from 'next-themes';
     6→import { getPostsBasePath } from '@/lib/urls';
     7→
     8→// Maps site locale codes to Giscus-supported language codes.
     9→// Full list: https://github.com/giscus/giscus/tree/main/locales
    10→const GISCUS_LANG: Record<string, string> = {
    11→  en: 'en',
    12→  zh: 'zh-CN',
    13→  'zh-TW': 'zh-TW',
    14→  ja: 'ja',
    15→  ko: 'ko',
    16→  de: 'de',
    17→  fr: 'fr',
    18→  es: 'es',
    19→  pt: 'pt',
    20→  ru: 'ru',
    21→};
    22→
    23→export default function Comments({ slug, postUrl }: { slug: string; postUrl?: string }) {
    24→  const { provider, giscus, disqus } = siteConfig.comments;
    25→  const { theme, systemTheme } = useTheme();
    26→
    27→  const currentTheme = theme === 'system' ? systemTheme : theme;
    28→  const giscusLang = GISCUS_LANG[siteConfig.i18n.defaultLocale] ?? siteConfig.i18n.defaultLocale;
    29→
    30→  if (provider === 'giscus' && giscus.repo) {
    31→    return (
    32→      <div className="mt-12 pt-12 border-t border-muted/20">
    33→        <Giscus
    34→          id="comments"
    35→          repo={giscus.repo as `${string}/${string}`}
    36→          repoId={giscus.repoId}
    37→          category={giscus.category}
    38→          categoryId={giscus.categoryId}
    39→          mapping="pathname"
    40→          term={slug}
    41→          reactionsEnabled="1"
    42→          emitMetadata="0"
    43→          inputPosition="top"
    44→          theme={currentTheme === 'dark' ? 'transparent_dark' : 'light'}
    45→          lang={giscusLang}
    46→          loading="lazy"
    47→        />
    48→      </div>
    49→    );
    50→  }
    51→
    52→  if (provider === 'disqus' && disqus.shortname) {
    53→    return (
    54→      <div className="mt-12 pt-12 border-t border-muted/20">
    55→        <div id="disqus_thread"></div>
    56→        <script
    57→          dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{
    58→            __html: `
    59→              var disqus_config = function () {
    60→                this.page.url = ${JSON.stringify(postUrl ?? `${siteConfig.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/${getPostsBasePath()}/${slug}`)};
    61→                this.page.identifier = ${JSON.stringify(slug)};
    62→              };
    63→              (function() {
    64→                var d = document, s = d.createElement('script');
    65→                s.src = 'https://${disqus.shortname}.disqus.com/embed.js';
    66→                s.setAttribute('data-timestamp', +new Date());
    67→                (d.head || d.body).appendChild(s);
    68→              })();
    69→            `,
    70→          }}
    71→        />
    72→        <noscript>
    73→          Please enable JavaScript to view the <a href="https://disqus.com/?ref_noscript">comments powered by Disqus.</a>
    74→        </noscript>
    75→      </div>
    76→    );
    77→  }
    78→
    79→  return null;
    80→}
    81→
```

```
    10→
    11→export function generateStaticParams() {
    12→  if (siteConfig.features?.flow?.enabled === false) return [{ slug: '_' }];
    13→  const notes = getAllNotes();
    14→  if (notes.length === 0) return [{ slug: '_' }];
    15→  return notes.map(note => ({ slug: note.slug }));
    16→}
    17→
    18→export const dynamicParams = false;
    19→
    20→export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
    21→  const { slug } = await params;
    22→  const note = getNoteBySlug(slug);
    23→  if (!note) return { title: 'Not Found' };
    24→  return {
    25→    title: `${note.title} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    26→    description: note.excerpt,
    27→    openGraph: {
    28→      title: note.title,
    29→      description: note.excerpt,
    30→      type: 'article',
    31→      publishedTime: note.date,
    32→      siteName: resolveLocale(siteConfig.title),
    33→    },
    34→  };
    35→}
    36→

    61→  return (
    62→    <div className="layout-main">

    66→        ? 'grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[280px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-8 items-start'

   102→
```

```
     1→import { getPostBySlug, getAllPosts, getRelatedPosts, getSeriesPosts, getSeriesData, getAdjacentPosts, buildSlugRegistry, getBacklinks, PostData } from '@/lib/markdown';
     2→import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
     3→import PostLayout from '@/layouts/PostLayout';
     4→import SimpleLayout from '@/layouts/SimpleLayout';
     5→import { Metadata } from 'next';
     6→import { siteConfig } from '../../../../site.config';
     7→import { resolveLocale } from '@/lib/i18n';
     8→import { getPostsBasePath, getPostUrl } from '@/lib/urls';
     9→import { buildPostJsonLd, serializeJsonLd, resolveImageUrl } from '@/lib/json-ld';
    10→
    11→function safeDecodeParam(param: string): string {
    12→  try {
    13→    return decodeURIComponent(param);
    14→  } catch {
    15→    return param;
    16→  }
    17→}
    18→
    19→function resolvePostFromParam(rawSlug: string) {
    20→  const decoded = safeDecodeParam(rawSlug);
    21→  return (
    22→    getPostBySlug(decoded) ||
    23→    getPostBySlug(rawSlug) ||
    24→    getPostBySlug(decoded.normalize('NFC')) ||
    25→    getPostBySlug(decoded.normalize('NFD'))
    26→  );
    27→}
    28→
    29→/**
    30→ * Generates the static paths for all blog posts at build time.
    31→ * This ensures fast page loads and SEO optimization.
    32→ */
    33→export async function generateStaticParams() {
    34→  if (getPostsBasePath() !== 'posts') return [{ slug: '_' }]; // Route disabled; custom path handles this
    35→  const posts = getAllPosts();
    36→  if (posts.length === 0) return [{ slug: '_' }];
    37→  // Work around Next dev static-param checks for percent-encoded Unicode paths
    38→  // under `output: "export"` by including encoded variants only in development.
    39→  // Production export keeps raw segment values.
    40→  const slugs = new Set<string>();
    41→  for (const post of posts) {
    42→    slugs.add(post.slug);
    43→    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
    44→      slugs.add(encodeURIComponent(post.slug));
    45→    }
    46→  }
    47→  return Array.from(slugs).map((slug) => ({ slug }));
    48→}
    49→
    50→export const dynamicParams = false;
    51→
    52→export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
    53→  const { slug: rawSlug } = await params;
    54→  const post = resolvePostFromParam(rawSlug);
    55→
    56→  if (!post) {
    57→    return {
    58→      title: 'Post Not Found',
    59→    };
    60→  }
    61→
    62→  const siteUrl = siteConfig.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '');
    63→  const ogImage = resolveImageUrl(post.coverImage, siteConfig.ogImage, siteUrl);
    64→
    65→  return {
    66→    title: `${post.title} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    67→    description: post.excerpt,
    68→    openGraph: {
    69→      title: post.title,
    70→      description: post.excerpt,
    71→      type: 'article',
    72→      publishedTime: post.date,
    73→      authors: post.authors,
    74→      images: [
    75→        {
    76→          url: ogImage,
    77→          width: 1200,
    78→          height: 630,
    79→          alt: post.title,
    80→        },
    81→      ],
    82→      siteName: resolveLocale(siteConfig.title),
    83→    },
    84→    twitter: {
    85→      card: 'summary_large_image',
    86→      title: post.title,
    87→      description: post.excerpt,
    88→      images: [ogImage],
    89→    },
    90→  };
    91→}
    92→
    93→export default async function PostPage({
    94→  params,
    95→}: {
    96→  params: Promise<{ slug: string }>;
    97→}) {
    98→  const { slug: rawSlug } = await params;
    99→  const slug = safeDecodeParam(rawSlug);
   100→  const post = resolvePostFromParam(rawSlug);
   101→
   102→  if (!post) {
   103→    notFound();
   104→  }
   105→
   106→  // Determine layout based on frontmatter
   107→  const layout = post.layout || 'post';
   108→
   109→  const siteUrl = siteConfig.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '');
   110→  const jsonLd = buildPostJsonLd({
   111→    post,
   112→    postUrl: `${siteUrl}${getPostUrl(post)}`,
   113→    siteTitle: resolveLocale(siteConfig.title),
   114→    siteUrl,
   115→    defaultOgImage: siteConfig.ogImage,
   116→  });
   117→  const jsonLdScript = <script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: serializeJsonLd(jsonLd) }} />;
   118→
   119→  if (layout === 'simple') {
   120→    return <>{jsonLdScript}<SimpleLayout post={post} /></>;
   121→  }
   122→
   123→  const relatedPosts = getRelatedPosts(slug);
   124→  const { prev, next } = getAdjacentPosts(slug);
   125→  const slugRegistry = buildSlugRegistry();
   126→  const backlinks = getBacklinks(slug);
   127→  let seriesPosts: PostData[] = [];
   128→  let seriesTitle: string | undefined;
   129→
   130→  if (post.series) {
   131→    seriesPosts = getSeriesPosts(post.series);
   132→    const seriesData = getSeriesData(post.series);
   133→    seriesTitle = seriesData?.title;
   134→  }
   135→
   136→  return (
   137→    <>
   138→      {jsonLdScript}
   139→      <PostLayout post={post} relatedPosts={relatedPosts} seriesPosts={seriesPosts} seriesTitle={seriesTitle} prevPost={prev} nextPost={next} backlinks={backlinks} slugRegistry={slugRegistry} />
   140→    </>
   141→  );
   142→}
```

```
    30→export default function PostLayout({ post, relatedPosts, seriesPosts, seriesTitle, prevPost, nextPost, backlinks, slugRegistry }: PostLayoutProps) {
    31→  const showToc = siteConfig.posts?.toc !== false && post.toc !== false && post.headings && post.headings.length > 0;

    34→  const postUrl = `${siteConfig.baseUrl}${getPostUrl(post)}`;

    40→        ? 'grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[280px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-8 items-start'

    42→      }>

    45→          <PostSidebar
    46→            seriesSlug={hasSeries ? post.series : undefined}
    47→            seriesTitle={hasSeries ? (seriesTitle || post.series) : undefined}
    48→            posts={hasSeries ? seriesPosts : undefined}
    49→            currentSlug={post.slug}
    50→            headings={showToc ? post.headings : []}
    51→            shareUrl={postUrl}
    52→            shareTitle={post.title}
    53→          />
    54→        )}
    55→
    56→        <article className="min-w-0 w-full max-w-3xl mx-auto overflow-x-hidden">
    57→          <header className="mb-16 border-b border-muted/10 pb-8">
    58→            {post.draft && (
    59→              <div className="mb-4">
    60→                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-red-500 bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/30 px-2 py-1 rounded tracking-widest inline-block">
    61→                  DRAFT
    62→                </span>
    63→              </div>
    64→            )}

    71→              <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-muted/30" />
```

```
    10→import TocPanel from './TocPanel';

    14→interface PostSidebarProps {
    15→  seriesSlug?: string;

    24→
    25→function getVisibleIndices(total: number, current: number): (number | 'ellipsis')[] {
    26→  if (total <= 7) return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => i);
    27→  const result: (number | 'ellipsis')[] = [];
    28→  result.push(0);
    29→  const windowStart = Math.max(1, current - 2);
    30→  const windowEnd = Math.min(total - 2, current + 2);
    31→  if (windowStart > 1) result.push('ellipsis');
    32→  for (let i = windowStart; i <= windowEnd; i++) result.push(i);
    33→  if (windowEnd < total - 2) result.push('ellipsis');
    34→  result.push(total - 1);
    35→  return result;
    36→}
    37→
    38→export default function PostSidebar({ seriesSlug, seriesTitle, posts, currentSlug, headings, localeHeadings, shareUrl, shareTitle }: PostSidebarProps) {
    39→  const { t, language } = useLanguage();
    40→  const activeHeadings = localeHeadings?.[language] ?? headings;
    41→  const hasSeries = !!(seriesSlug && posts && posts.length > 0);
    42→  const currentIndex = hasSeries ? posts!.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug) : -1;
    43→  // Chronological sort (ascending date) — used for both progress counter and isPast styling
    44→  const sortedPosts = hasSeries
    45→    ? [...posts!].sort((a, b) => new Date(a.date).getTime() - new Date(b.date).getTime())
    46→    : null;
    47→  const progressIndex = hasSeries ? sortedPosts!.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug) : -1;
    48→  const currentItemRef = useRef<HTMLLIElement>(null);
    49→  const sidebarRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
    50→  const [seriesCollapsed, setSeriesCollapsed] = useState(false);
    51→  useSidebarAutoScroll(sidebarRef, currentItemRef, currentSlug);
    52→
    53→  return (
    54→    <aside
    55→      ref={sidebarRef}
    56→      data-testid="post-sidebar"
    57→      className="hidden lg:block sticky top-20 self-start w-[280px] max-h-[calc(100vh-6rem)] overflow-y-auto pr-4 scrollbar-hide hover:scrollbar-thin"
    58→    >
    59→      {/* TOC — always at top */}
    60→      <TocPanel
    61→        headings={activeHeadings}
    62→        className={`mb-6 ${hasSeries ? 'pb-4 border-b border-muted/10' : ''}`}
    63→      />
    64→
    65→      {/* Series section — below TOC */}
    66→      {hasSeries && (
    67→        <div>
    68→          {/* Header — always visible */}
    69→          <div className="mb-3">
    70→            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
    71→              <span className="text-[10px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-accent">
    72→                {t('series')}
    73→              </span>
    74→              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-muted/60">
    75→                {progressIndex >= 0 ? progressIndex + 1 : '?'} / {posts!.length}
    76→              </span>
    77→            </div>
    78→            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
    79→              <Link href={`/series/${seriesSlug}`} className="group block no-underline flex-1 min-w-0">
    80→                <h3 className="font-serif font-bold text-heading text-base leading-snug group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">
    81→                  {seriesTitle}
    82→                </h3>
    83→              </Link>
    84→              <button
    85→                onClick={() => setSeriesCollapsed(prev => !prev)}
    86→                className="flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-muted hover:text-foreground transition-colors"
    87→                aria-label={seriesCollapsed ? 'Expand series' : 'Collapse series'}
    88→              >
    89→                <svg
    90→                  className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 ${seriesCollapsed ? '' : 'rotate-180'}`}
    91→                  fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}
    92→                >
    93→                  <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7" />
    94→                </svg>
    95→              </button>
    96→            </div>
    97→          </div>
    98→
    99→          {/* Collapsible: post list + footer link */}
   100→          {!seriesCollapsed && (
   101→            <>
   102→              <nav aria-label="Series navigation" className="mb-4 animate-slide-down">
   103→                <ul className="space-y-1 relative before:absolute before:left-[11px] before:top-3 before:bottom-3 before:w-px before:bg-muted/15">
   104→                  {getVisibleIndices(posts!.length, currentIndex).map((item, i) => {
   105→                    if (item === 'ellipsis') {
   106→                      return (
   107→                        <li key={`ellipsis-${i}`} className="flex items-center py-1 pl-3">
   108→                          <span className="text-xs font-mono text-muted/40 tracking-widest">···</span>
   109→                        </li>
   110→                      );
   111→                    }

   116→

   131→                                : 'bg-muted/10 text-muted group-hover:bg-muted/20 group-hover:text-foreground'
   132→                          }`}>
   133→                            {padNumber(item + 1)}

   136→                            <span className={`block text-sm leading-snug transition-colors ${

   142→                            }`}>
   143→                              {post.title}
```

Now I have a clear picture. Here's my analysis:

**Current state:**

| Feature | Post page | Flow page | Note page |
|---|---|---|---|
| Comments | ✅ (`Comments` in `PostLayout`) | ❌ missing | ❌ missing |
| Share | ✅ in sidebar (`PostSidebar`) | ⚠️ inline at bottom (`ShareBar` on line 105) | ❌ missing |
| Tag variant | `default` (pill style) | `compact` (plain text) | `default` (pill style) |

**Proposed changes:**

1. **Tag style** — Flow page uses `variant="compact"` while Notes uses `variant="default"`. Simple fix: change `variant="compact"` to `variant="default"` in the Flow detail page. (`FlowTimelineEntry` in the listing already uses compact, which is fine for the list view.)

2. **ShareBar in sidebar** — For the Note page, add ShareBar at the bottom of `NoteSidebar` (same pattern as `PostSidebar` lines 167–174). For the Flow page, move the inline ShareBar up into `FlowCalendarSidebar` by passing `shareUrl`/`shareTitle` props — or alternatively keep it inline but display it only when not in sidebar view (mobile vs desktop).

3. **Comments** — Add `<Comments>` to both Flow and Note detail pages, after the content/navigation, similar to PostLayout. The `Comments` component already accepts a `slug` and optional `postUrl`, so it just needs to be imported and placed.

**One design question:** For the Flow sidebar specifically — `FlowCalendarSidebar` is a calendar/navigation component. Placing ShareBar there might feel a bit odd semantically. Alternative: add a small share section at the top of the article sidebar area, or keep it inline but only on mobile (hide on desktop). The Note sidebar (`NoteSidebar`) is a better fit since it's already a general-purpose aside.

Do you want to go ahead with all three changes? And for the Flow share, should we put it in `FlowCalendarSidebar` or keep it inline?

```
     1→import fs from 'fs';
     2→import path from 'path';
     3→
     4→// Usage (auto-scan — recommended):
     5→//   bun run new-flow-from-chat                         # import all new files in imports/chats/
     6→//   bun run new-flow-from-chat --all                   # re-import every file (ignore history)
     7→//   bun run new-flow-from-chat --dry-run               # preview without writing
     8→//   bun run new-flow-from-chat --author "Alice"        # only include Alice's messages
     9→//   bun run new-flow-from-chat --append                # append to existing flow files
    10→//   bun run new-flow-from-chat --timestamp             # include timestamps (default: excluded)
    11→//
    12→// Usage (explicit file):
    13→//   bun run new-flow-from-chat <file>                  # process one specific file
    14→//
    15→// Import history is tracked in imports/chats/.imported (one filename per line).
    16→// That file is gitignored along with the rest of imports/.
    17→//
    18→// Input format (one message block per entry):
    19→//   username YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss
    20→//   message line 1
    21→//   message line 2
    22→//   ...
    23→//
    24→// Output: content/flows/YYYY/MM/DD.md  (one file per calendar day)
    25→
    26→const CHATS_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), 'imports', 'chats');
    27→const IMPORTED_RECORD = path.join(CHATS_DIR, '.imported');
    28→
    29→// ── Arg parsing ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    30→
    31→const args = process.argv.slice(2);
    32→
    33→// Collect positional args, skipping values that follow named flags
    34→const positional: string[] = [];
    35→for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
    36→  if (args[i] === '--author') { i++; continue; }
    37→  if (!args[i].startsWith('--')) positional.push(args[i]);
    38→}
    39→
    40→const explicitFile      = positional[0] ?? null;
    41→const authorIdx         = args.indexOf('--author');
    42→const filterAuthor      = authorIdx > -1 ? args[authorIdx + 1] : null;
    43→const dryRun            = args.includes('--dry-run');
    44→const appendMode        = args.includes('--append');
    45→const reimportAll       = args.includes('--all');
    46→const includeTimestamp  = args.includes('--timestamp');
    47→
    48→// ── Types ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    49→
    50→interface Message {
    51→  username: string;
    52→  date: string;   // YYYY-MM-DD
    53→  time: string;   // HH:mm:ss
    54→  lines: string[];
    55→}
    56→
    57→// ── Parser ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    58→
    59→// Matches: "username YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss" (username may contain spaces)
    60→const HEADER_RE = /^(.+?)\s+(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\s+(\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2})$/;
    61→
    62→function parseChat(raw: string): Message[] {
    63→  const messages: Message[] = [];
    64→  let current: Message | null = null;
    65→
    66→  for (const rawLine of raw.split('\n')) {
    67→    const line = rawLine.replace(/\r$/, ''); // strip Windows line endings
    68→    const match = line.match(HEADER_RE);
    69→    if (match) {
    70→      if (current) messages.push(current);
    71→      current = { username: match[1].trim(), date: match[2], time: match[3], lines: [] };
    72→    } else if (current) {
    73→      current.lines.push(line);
    74→    }
    75→    // Lines before the first header are silently ignored
    76→  }
    77→
    78→  if (current) messages.push(current);
    79→  return messages;
    80→}
    81→
    82→// ── Rendering ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    83→
    84→function renderBlock(msg: Message, showUsername: boolean, showTime: boolean): string {
    85→  const content = msg.lines.join('\n').trimEnd();
    86→  if (!content.trim()) return ''; // skip empty messages
    87→
    88→  const headerParts: string[] = [];
    89→  if (showUsername) headerParts.push(`**${msg.username}**`);
    90→  if (showTime) headerParts.push(msg.time);
    91→
    92→  if (headerParts.length === 0) return content;
    93→  return `${headerParts.join(' · ')}\n\n${content}`;
    94→}
    95→
    96→function renderFlow(messages: Message[], showUsername: boolean, showTime: boolean): string {
    97→  const blocks = messages.map(m => renderBlock(m, showUsername, showTime)).filter(Boolean);
    98→  return `---\ntags: []\n---\n\n${blocks.join('\n\n---\n\n')}\n`;
    99→}
   100→
   101→// ── Import tracking ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   102→
   103→function loadImported(): Set<string> {
   104→  if (!fs.existsSync(IMPORTED_RECORD)) return new Set();
   105→  return new Set(
   106→    fs.readFileSync(IMPORTED_RECORD, 'utf8')
   107→      .split('\n').map(l => l.trim()).filter(Boolean),
   108→  );
   109→}
   110→
   111→function markImported(filename: string): void {
   112→  fs.appendFileSync(IMPORTED_RECORD, filename + '\n');
   113→}
   114→
   115→// ── File discovery ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   116→
   117→function findPendingFiles(): string[] {
   118→  if (!fs.existsSync(CHATS_DIR)) {
   119→    console.error(`Directory not found: ${CHATS_DIR}`);
   120→    console.error('Create it (or run: mkdir -p imports/chats) and drop chat export files there.');
   121→    process.exit(1);
   122→  }
   123→  const imported = reimportAll ? new Set<string>() : loadImported();
   124→  return fs.readdirSync(CHATS_DIR)
   125→    .filter(name => !name.startsWith('.') && /\.(txt|log)$/i.test(name))
   126→    .filter(name => !imported.has(name))
   127→    .sort()
   128→    .map(name => path.join(CHATS_DIR, name));
   129→}
   130→
   131→// ── Process one file ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   132→
   133→function processFile(filePath: string): boolean {
   134→  const raw = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
   135→  const allMessages = parseChat(raw);
   136→
   137→  if (allMessages.length === 0) {
   138→    console.log(`  ⚠  no messages found — check the file format`);
   139→    return false;
   140→  }
   141→
   142→  const messages = filterAuthor
   143→    ? allMessages.filter(m => m.username.toLowerCase() === filterAuthor.toLowerCase())
   144→    : allMessages;
   145→
   146→  if (messages.length === 0) {
   147→    console.log(`  ⚠  no messages from "${filterAuthor}" found`);
   148→    return false;
   149→  }
   150→
   151→  const byDate = new Map<string, Message[]>();
   152→  for (const msg of messages) {
   153→    const list = byDate.get(msg.date) ?? [];
   154→    list.push(msg);
   155→    byDate.set(msg.date, list);
   156→  }
   157→
   158→  const showUsername = filterAuthor === null;
   159→  const showTime = includeTimestamp;
   160→  const flowsDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'content', 'flows');
   161→  let created = 0, appended = 0, skipped = 0;
   162→
   163→  for (const [date, dayMsgs] of [...byDate.entries()].sort()) {
   164→    const [year, month, day] = date.split('-');
   165→    const dirPath = path.join(flowsDir, year, month);
   166→    const mdPath  = path.join(dirPath, `${day}.md`);
   167→    const mdxPath = path.join(dirPath, `${day}.mdx`);
   168→    const existing = fs.existsSync(mdPath) ? mdPath : fs.existsSync(mdxPath) ? mdxPath : null;
   169→    const flowContent = renderFlow(dayMsgs, showUsername, showTime);
   170→
   171→    if (dryRun) {
   172→      const label = `${date} (${dayMsgs.length} msg${dayMsgs.length === 1 ? '' : 's'})`;
   173→      console.log(`    ── ${label} → ${path.relative(process.cwd(), mdPath)}`);
   174→      console.log(flowContent);
   175→      continue;
   176→    }
   177→
   178→    if (existing && !appendMode) {
   179→      console.log(`    ⚠  ${date}: flow already exists — skipped (use --append to add)`);
   180→      skipped++;
   181→      continue;
   182→    }
   183→
   184→    if (!fs.existsSync(dirPath)) fs.mkdirSync(dirPath, { recursive: true });
   185→
   186→    if (existing) {
   187→      const blocks = flowContent.replace(/^---[\s\S]*?---\n\n/, '').trimEnd();
   188→      fs.appendFileSync(existing, `\n\n---\n\n${blocks}\n`);
   189→      console.log(`    +  ${date}: appended ${dayMsgs.length} msg${dayMsgs.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} → ${path.basename(existing)}`);
   190→      appended++;
   191→    } else {
   192→      fs.writeFileSync(mdPath, flowContent);
   193→      console.log(`    ✓  ${date}: ${path.relative(process.cwd(), mdPath)} (${dayMsgs.length} msg${dayMsgs.length === 1 ? '' : 's'})`);
   194→      created++;
   195→    }
   196→  }
   197→
   198→  const parts: string[] = [];
   199→  if (created  > 0) parts.push(`${created} created`);
   200→  if (appended > 0) parts.push(`${appended} appended`);
   201→  if (skipped  > 0) parts.push(`${skipped} skipped`);
   202→  const summary = dryRun ? 'dry run' : (parts.join(', ') || 'nothing to do');
   203→  console.log(`    → ${summary} · ${messages.length} msg${messages.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} across ${byDate.size} day${byDate.size === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
   204→
   205→  return true;
   206→}
   207→
   208→// ── Main ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   209→
   210→if (explicitFile) {
   211→  if (!fs.existsSync(explicitFile)) {
   212→    console.error(`Error: "${explicitFile}" not found.`);
   213→    process.exit(1);
   214→  }
   215→  console.log(`Processing: ${explicitFile}${dryRun ? ' (dry run)' : ''}`);
   216→  processFile(explicitFile);
   217→} else {
   218→  const pending = findPendingFiles();
   219→
   220→  if (pending.length === 0) {
   221→    const hint = reimportAll ? '' : ' (run with --all to re-import everything)';
   222→    console.log(`Nothing new to import in imports/chats/${hint}.`);
   223→    process.exit(0);
   224→  }
   225→
   226→  const modeLabel = dryRun ? ' (dry run)' : reimportAll ? ' (--all)' : '';
   227→  console.log(`Found ${pending.length} file${pending.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} to import${modeLabel}:`);
   228→
   229→  for (const filePath of pending) {
   230→    console.log(`\n  ${path.basename(filePath)}`);
   231→    const ok = processFile(filePath);
   232→    if (!dryRun && ok) markImported(path.basename(filePath));
   233→  }
   234→
   235→  if (!dryRun) {
   236→    console.log(`\nDone. Import history saved to ${path.relative(process.cwd(), IMPORTED_RECORD)}.`);
   237→  }
   238→}
   239→
```

> AGENT

Good fit for the project. Here's my assessment:

**Natural mapping:**
- Obsidian daily notes (`YYYY-MM-DD.md`) → `content/flows/YYYY/MM/DD.md`
- Obsidian regular notes → `content/notes/[slug].md`

**What works out of the box:**
- Wikilinks (`[[Note Title]]`) — Amytis's `MarkdownRenderer` already resolves these via `slugRegistry`, so no conversion needed
- Markdown content — passes through unchanged
- Tags in frontmatter — just need remapping

**What needs handling:**

| Challenge | Notes |
|---|---|
| Frontmatter remapping | Obsidian uses `created`/`modified`; Amytis needs `date` (flows don't even need it — it's inferred from the path) |
| Inline `#tag` → frontmatter tags | Obsidian lets you write `#tag` in body; these need to be extracted and moved to frontmatter |
| Obsidian tag format | Can be `tags: [a, b]` or `tags:\n  - a` — gray-matter handles both fine |
| Slug generation for notes | Filename is the note title; needs slugification (same logic as `new-note.ts`) |
| Conflict handling | If a flow date already exists: skip or `--append` |
| Images/embeds (`![[file.png]]`) | Attachments need to be copied to the right `content/` path |

**Proposed approach:** A single `scripts/import-obsidian.ts` following the exact same pattern as `new-flow-from-chat.ts` — reads from `imports/obsidian/`, writes to `content/`, tracks history in `.imported`, supports `--dry-run` / `--all` / `--append`. Two modes:
- `--type flows` — processes daily notes (detects `YYYY-MM-DD.md` filenames)
- `--type notes` — processes regular notes
- Default: auto-detect based on filename pattern

**Before I implement, a few questions:**

1. What does your Obsidian daily notes folder look like — flat `YYYY-MM-DD.md` files, or nested by year/month?
2. What frontmatter fields do your notes typically have? (e.g., do you use `created`, `date`, `aliases`?)
3. Do you want inline `#tags` in body content extracted to frontmatter, or left as-is?
4. Do you have images/attachments to handle, or mostly text?

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/amytis-claude-review/scripts/import-obsidian.ts",
  "content": "import fs from 'fs';\nimport path from 'path';\nimport matter from 'gray-matter';\n\n// Usage:\n//   bun run import-obsidian                   # import all new files from imports/obsidian/\n//   bun run import-obsidian --dry-run         # preview without writing\n//   bun run import-obsidian --all             # re-import everything (ignore history)\n//   bun run import-obsidian --append          # append to existing flows instead of skipping\n//   bun run import-obsidian --flows-only      # only process YYYY-MM-DD.md daily notes\n//   bun run import-obsidian --notes-only      # only process regular notes\n//   bun run import-obsidian <file>            # process one specific file\n//\n// File routing:\n//   YYYY-MM-DD.md  →  content/flows/YYYY/MM/DD.md\n//   other .md      →  content/notes/[slug].md\n//\n// Inline Obsidian #tags are extracted from body content and moved to frontmatter.\n// Import history is tracked in imports/obsidian/.imported (one filename per line).\n\nconst OBSIDIAN_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), 'imports', 'obsidian');\nconst FLOWS_DIR    = path.join(process.cwd(), 'content', 'flows');\nconst NOTES_DIR    = path.join(process.cwd(), 'content', 'notes');\nconst IMPORTED_RECORD = path.join(OBSIDIAN_DIR, '.imported');\n\n// ── Arg parsing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nconst args = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst positional   = args.filter(a => !a.startsWith('--'));\nconst explicitFile = positional[0] ?? null;\nconst dryRun       = args.includes('--dry-run');\nconst reimportAll  = args.includes('--all');\nconst appendMode   = args.includes('--append');\nconst flowsOnly    = args.includes('--flows-only');\nconst notesOnly    = args.includes('--notes-only');\n\nconst DATE_FILE_RE = /^(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})\\.mdx?$/;\n\n// ── Tag extraction ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// Extracts Obsidian inline #tags from body, removes them, and returns both.\n// Skips fenced code blocks to avoid matching #include, #define, etc.\nfunction extractInlineTags(body: string): { body: string; tags: string[] } {\n  const found = new Set<string>();\n\n  // Split on fenced code blocks; only process non-code segments.\n  const segments = body.split(/(```[\\s\\S]*?```|`[^`]+`)/g);\n\n  const processed = segments.map((seg, i) => {\n    // Odd indices are code spans/blocks — leave them untouched.\n    if (i % 2 !== 0) return seg;\n\n    // Match #tag at start of line or after whitespace.\n    // Capture prefix (whitespace or empty) separately so we can preserve it.\n    return seg.replace(\n      /(^|\\s)#([a-zA-Z\\u4e00-\\u9fff][a-zA-Z0-9\\u4e00-\\u9fff/_-]*)/gm,\n      (_, prefix, tag) => {\n        found.add(tag.replace(/\\//g, '-').toLowerCase());\n        return prefix; // keep the whitespace, drop the #tag\n      },\n    );\n  });\n\n  // Collapse runs of blank lines left after tag removal.\n  const cleaned = processed.join('').replace(/\\n{3,}/g, '\\n\\n').trim();\n  return { body: cleaned, tags: [...found].sort() };\n}\n\n// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction slugify(title: string): string {\n  return title\n    .toLowerCase()\n    .replace(/[^a-z0-9\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+/g, '-')\n    .replace(/^-|-$/g, '');\n}\n\nfunction formatDate(d: Date): string {\n  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];\n}\n\nfunction tagsLiteral(tags: string[]): string {\n  return tags.length > 0 ? `[${tags.map(t => `\"${t}\"`).join(', ')}]` : '[]';\n}\n\n// ── Import tracking ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction loadImported(): Set<string> {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(IMPORTED_RECORD)) return new Set();\n  return new Set(\n    fs.readFileSync(IMPORTED_RECORD, 'utf8').split('\\n').map(l => l.trim()).filter(Boolean),\n  );\n}\n\nfunction markImported(filename: string): void {\n  fs.appendFileSync(IMPORTED_RECORD, filename + '\\n');\n}\n\n// ── Flow processor ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction processFlow(filePath: string, filename: string): boolean {\n  const match = filename.match(DATE_FILE_RE)!;\n  const [, year, month, day] = match;\n  const date = `${year}-${month}-${day}`;\n\n  const raw = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');\n  const parsed = matter(raw);\n\n  const existingTags: string[] = Array.isArray(parsed.data.tags) ? parsed.data.tags.map(String) : [];\n  const { body, tags: inlineTags } = extractInlineTags(parsed.content);\n  const tags = [...new Set([...existingTags.map(t => t.toLowerCase()), ...inlineTags])].sort();\n\n  const outDir  = path.join(FLOWS_DIR, year, month);\n  const outPath = path.join(outDir, `${day}.md`);\n  const altPath = path.join(outDir, `${day}.mdx`);\n  const existing = fs.existsSync(outPath) ? outPath : fs.existsSync(altPath) ? altPath : null;\n\n  const flowContent = `---\\ntags: ${tagsLiteral(tags)}\\n---\\n\\n${body}\\n`;\n\n  if (dryRun) {\n    console.log(`    [flow] ${date} → content/flows/${year}/${month}/${day}.md`);\n    if (tags.length > 0) console.log(`           tags: ${tags.join(', ')}`);\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  if (existing && !appendMode) {\n    console.log(`    ⚠  ${date}: flow already exists — skipped (use --append to add)`);\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  if (!fs.existsSync(outDir)) fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });\n\n  if (existing) {\n    fs.appendFileSync(existing, `\\n\\n---\\n\\n${body}\\n`);\n    console.log(`    +  ${date}: appended → ${path.basename(existing)}`);\n  } else {\n    fs.writeFileSync(outPath, flowContent);\n    console.log(`    ✓  ${date}: content/flows/${year}/${month}/${day}.md`);\n  }\n\n  return true;\n}\n\n// ── Note processor ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction processNote(filePath: string, filename: string): boolean {\n  const titleFromFile = path.basename(filename, path.extname(filename));\n  const slug = slugify(titleFromFile);\n\n  const outPath = path.join(NOTES_DIR, `${slug}.md`);\n  const altPath = path.join(NOTES_DIR, `${slug}.mdx`);\n  const existing = fs.existsSync(outPath) ? outPath : fs.existsSync(altPath) ? altPath : null;\n\n  const raw    = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');\n  const stat   = fs.statSync(filePath);\n  const parsed = matter(raw);\n\n  const title   = (parsed.data.title as string | undefined) ?? titleFromFile;\n  const date    = (parsed.data.date as string | undefined)\n               ?? (parsed.data.created as string | undefined)\n               ?? formatDate(stat.mtime);\n  const existingTags: string[] = Array.isArray(parsed.data.tags) ? parsed.data.tags.map(String) : [];\n  const aliases: string[]      = Array.isArray(parsed.data.aliases) ? parsed.data.aliases.map(String) : [];\n\n  const { body, tags: inlineTags } = extractInlineTags(parsed.content);\n  const tags = [...new Set([...existingTags.map(t => t.toLowerCase()), ...inlineTags])].sort();\n\n  const escapedTitle = title.replace(/\"/g, '\\\\\"');\n  const noteContent = [\n    '---',\n    `title: \"${escapedTitle}\"`,\n    `date: \"${date}\"`,\n    `tags: ${tagsLiteral(tags)}`,\n    `aliases: ${tagsLiteral(aliases)}`,\n    '---',\n    '',\n    body,\n    '',\n  ].join('\\n');\n\n  if (dryRun) {\n    console.log(`    [note] \"${title}\" → content/notes/${slug}.md`);\n    if (tags.length > 0) console.log(`           tags: ${tags.join(', ')}`);\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  if (existing) {\n    console.log(`    ⚠  \"${slug}\": note already exists — skipped`);\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  if (!fs.existsSync(NOTES_DIR)) fs.mkdirSync(NOTES_DIR, { recursive: true });\n  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, noteContent);\n  console.log(`    ✓  \"${title}\": content/notes/${slug}.md`);\n  return true;\n}\n\n// ── Dispatch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction processFile(filePath: string): boolean {\n  const filename = path.basename(filePath);\n  const isFlow = DATE_FILE_RE.test(filename);\n  if (isFlow && notesOnly)  return false;\n  if (!isFlow && flowsOnly) return false;\n  return isFlow ? processFlow(filePath, filename) : processNote(filePath, filename);\n}\n\n// ── File discovery ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction findPendingFiles(): string[] {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(OBSIDIAN_DIR)) {\n    console.error(`Directory not found: ${OBSIDIAN_DIR}`);\n    console.error('Create it and place your Obsidian exports there:');\n    console.error('  mkdir -p imports/obsidian');\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  const imported = reimportAll ? new Set<string>() : loadImported();\n  return fs.readdirSync(OBSIDIAN_DIR)\n    .filter(name => !name.startsWith('.') && /\\.mdx?$/.test(name))\n    .filter(name => !imported.has(name))\n    .sort()\n    .map(name => path.join(OBSIDIAN_DIR, name));\n}\n\n// ── Main ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nif (explicitFile) {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(explicitFile)) {\n    console.error(`Error: \"${explicitFile}\" not found.`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  console.log(`Processing: ${explicitFile}${dryRun ? ' (dry run)' : ''}`);\n  processFile(explicitFile);\n} else {\n  const pending = findPendingFiles();\n\n  if (pending.length === 0) {\n    const hint = reimportAll ? '' : ' (run with --all to re-import everything)';\n    console.log(`Nothing new to import in imports/obsidian/${hint}.`);\n    process.exit(0);\n  }\n\n  const modeLabel = dryRun ? ' (dry run)' : reimportAll ? ' (--all)' : '';\n  console.log(`Found ${pending.length} file${pending.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} to import${modeLabel}:`);\n\n  let flows = 0, notes = 0, skipped = 0;\n\n  for (const filePath of pending) {\n    console.log(`\\n  ${path.basename(filePath)}`);\n    const ok = processFile(filePath);\n    if (ok && !dryRun) {\n      markImported(path.basename(filePath));\n      DATE_FILE_RE.test(path.basename(filePath)) ? flows++ : notes++;\n    } else if (!ok) {\n      skipped++;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const parts: string[] = [];\n  if (flows   > 0) parts.push(`${flows} flow${flows === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);\n  if (notes   > 0) parts.push(`${notes} note${notes === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);\n  if (skipped > 0) parts.push(`${skipped} skipped`);\n\n  if (!dryRun) {\n    console.log(`\\nDone: ${parts.join(', ') || 'nothing imported'}.`);\n    console.log(`Import history saved to ${path.relative(process.cwd(), IMPORTED_RECORD)}.`);\n  } else {\n    console.log(`\\nDry run complete: ${parts.join(', ') || 'nothing to import'}.`);\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. Here's what the script does:

**Usage:**
```bash
mkdir -p imports/obsidian
# copy your Obsidian exports into imports/obsidian/

bun run import-obsidian --dry-run      # preview
bun run import-obsidian                # import new files
bun run import-obsidian --all          # re-import everything
bun run import-obsidian --flows-only   # only daily notes
bun run import-obsidian --notes-only   # only regular notes
bun run import-obsidian --append       # append to existing flows
```

**Key behaviors:**

- `YYYY-MM-DD.md` → `content/flows/YYYY/MM/DD.md` with `tags: [...]` frontmatter
- Any other `.md` → `content/notes/[slug].md` with `title`, `date`, `tags`, `aliases`
- Inline `#tags` are extracted from body and moved to frontmatter; the occurrences in body are removed
- Code blocks are skipped during tag extraction (avoids matching `#include`, `#define`, etc.)
- Nested Obsidian tags (`#area/project`) are flattened to `area-project`
- Conflict handling: flows can use `--append`, notes are always skipped (slug collision)
- Import history tracked in `imports/obsidian/.imported` so re-running only picks up new files

> DEVELOPER

> AGENT

Done. Now you can point at your vault directly:

```bash
# Use your Obsidian vault folder directly
bun run import-obsidian --vault ~/Documents/ObsidianVault --dry-run

# Only import daily notes from a specific subfolder
bun run import-obsidian --vault ~/Documents/ObsidianVault/Daily --flows-only

# Only import regular notes
bun run import-obsidian --vault ~/Documents/ObsidianVault/Notes --notes-only
```

A few notes on the design:
- `--vault` accepts any absolute or relative path; it's resolved with `path.resolve()` so `~`-style paths work when the shell expands them
- Import history (`.imported`) is always stored in `imports/obsidian/` regardless of `--vault`, so re-runs against the same vault won't re-import files
- The vault directory is never modified — the script only reads from it

> AGENT

Done! Created commit **43e7441** with message:

```
feat: add import-obsidian script for importing Obsidian vaults

Adds bun run import-obsidian command to import Obsidian files:
- YYYY-MM-DD.md files (daily notes) → content/flows/YYYY/MM/DD.md
- Other .md files (notes) → content/notes/[slug].md

Features:
- Extracts inline Obsidian #tags from body content to frontmatter
- Skips code blocks during tag extraction (avoids #include, etc.)
- Supports --vault <path> to point at any directory
- Respects --dry-run, --all, --append, --flows-only, --notes-only flags
- Import history tracked in imports/obsidian/.imported
```

Working tree is clean. Ready to use `bun run import-obsidian` to import your Obsidian vault.

> DEVELOPER

For me, the Obsidian journals are in the 'journals' folder; however, some users may use another name or keep them in the root directory. The notes may also be in other directories or root.

> AGENT

The issue is the script currently only scans the flat root of `OBSIDIAN_DIR`. The fix is to make it **recursive** — since the routing logic is already filename-based (`YYYY-MM-DD.md` → flow, anything else → note), recursion handles all vault layouts automatically. The import history also needs to track relative paths instead of just filenames to avoid collisions across subfolders.

```
     1→import fs from 'fs';
     2→import path from 'path';
     3→import matter from 'gray-matter';
     4→
     5→// Usage:
     6→//   bun run import-obsidian                          # import all new files from imports/obsidian/
     7→//   bun run import-obsidian --vault <path>           # use a custom vault/folder instead
     8→//   bun run import-obsidian --dry-run                # preview without writing
     9→//   bun run import-obsidian --all                    # re-import everything (ignore history)
    10→//   bun run import-obsidian --append                 # append to existing flows instead of skipping
    11→//   bun run import-obsidian --flows-only             # only process YYYY-MM-DD.md daily notes
    12→//   bun run import-obsidian --notes-only             # only process regular notes
    13→//   bun run import-obsidian <file>                   # process one specific file
    14→//
    15→// File routing:
    16→//   YYYY-MM-DD.md  →  content/flows/YYYY/MM/DD.md
    17→//   other .md      →  content/notes/[slug].md
    18→//
    19→// Inline Obsidian #tags are extracted from body content and moved to frontmatter.
    20→// Import history is tracked in imports/obsidian/.imported (one filename per line).
    21→
    22→const DEFAULT_OBSIDIAN_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), 'imports', 'obsidian');
    23→const FLOWS_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), 'content', 'flows');
    24→const NOTES_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), 'content', 'notes');
    25→
    26→// ── Arg parsing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    27→
    28→const args = process.argv.slice(2);
    29→
    30→const vaultIdx   = args.indexOf('--vault');
    31→const vaultArg   = vaultIdx > -1 ? args[vaultIdx + 1] : null;
    32→
    33→const positional   = args.filter((a, i) => !a.startsWith('--') && args[i - 1] !== '--vault');
    34→const explicitFile = positional[0] ?? null;
    35→const dryRun       = args.includes('--dry-run');
    36→const reimportAll  = args.includes('--all');
    37→const appendMode   = args.includes('--append');
    38→const flowsOnly    = args.includes('--flows-only');
    39→const notesOnly    = args.includes('--notes-only');
    40→
    41→const OBSIDIAN_DIR    = vaultArg ? path.resolve(vaultArg) : DEFAULT_OBSIDIAN_DIR;
    42→const IMPORTED_RECORD = path.join(DEFAULT_OBSIDIAN_DIR, '.imported');
    43→
    44→const DATE_FILE_RE = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})\.mdx?$/;
    45→
    46→// ── Tag extraction ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    47→
    48→// Extracts Obsidian inline #tags from body, removes them, and returns both.
    49→// Skips fenced code blocks to avoid matching #include, #define, etc.
    50→function extractInlineTags(body: string): { body: string; tags: string[] } {
    51→  const found = new Set<string>();
    52→
    53→  // Split on fenced code blocks; only process non-code segments.
    54→  const segments = body.split(/(```[\s\S]*?```|`[^`]+`)/g);
    55→
    56→  const processed = segments.map((seg, i) => {
    57→    // Odd indices are code spans/blocks — leave them untouched.
    58→    if (i % 2 !== 0) return seg;
    59→
    60→    // Match #tag at start of line or after whitespace.
    61→    // Capture prefix (whitespace or empty) separately so we can preserve it.
    62→    return seg.replace(
    63→      /(^|\s)#([a-zA-Z\u4e00-\u9fff][a-zA-Z0-9\u4e00-\u9fff/_-]*)/gm,
    64→      (_, prefix, tag) => {
    65→        found.add(tag.replace(/\//g, '-').toLowerCase());
    66→        return prefix; // keep the whitespace, drop the #tag
    67→      },
    68→    );
    69→  });
    70→
    71→  // Collapse runs of blank lines left after tag removal.
    72→  const cleaned = processed.join('').replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n').trim();
    73→  return { body: cleaned, tags: [...found].sort() };
    74→}
    75→
    76→// ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    77→
    78→function slugify(title: string): string {
    79→  return title
    80→    .toLowerCase()
    81→    .replace(/[^a-z0-9\u4e00-\u9fff]+/g, '-')
    82→    .replace(/^-|-$/g, '');
    83→}
    84→
    85→function formatDate(d: Date): string {
    86→  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
    87→}
    88→
    89→function tagsLiteral(tags: string[]): string {
    90→  return tags.length > 0 ? `[${tags.map(t => `"${t}"`).join(', ')}]` : '[]';
    91→}
    92→
    93→// ── Import tracking ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    94→
    95→function loadImported(): Set<string> {
    96→  if (!fs.existsSync(IMPORTED_RECORD)) return new Set();
    97→  return new Set(
    98→    fs.readFileSync(IMPORTED_RECORD, 'utf8').split('\n').map(l => l.trim()).filter(Boolean),
    99→  );
   100→}
   101→
   102→function markImported(filename: string): void {
   103→  fs.appendFileSync(IMPORTED_RECORD, filename + '\n');
   104→}
   105→
   106→// ── Flow processor ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   107→
   108→function processFlow(filePath: string, filename: string): boolean {
   109→  const match = filename.match(DATE_FILE_RE)!;
   110→  const [, year, month, day] = match;
   111→  const date = `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
   112→
   113→  const raw = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
   114→  const parsed = matter(raw);
   115→
   116→  const existingTags: string[] = Array.isArray(parsed.data.tags) ? parsed.data.tags.map(String) : [];
   117→  const { body, tags: inlineTags } = extractInlineTags(parsed.content);
   118→  const tags = [...new Set([...existingTags.map(t => t.toLowerCase()), ...inlineTags])].sort();
   119→
   120→  const outDir  = path.join(FLOWS_DIR, year, month);
   121→  const outPath = path.join(outDir, `${day}.md`);
   122→  const altPath = path.join(outDir, `${day}.mdx`);
   123→  const existing = fs.existsSync(outPath) ? outPath : fs.existsSync(altPath) ? altPath : null;
   124→
   125→  const flowContent = `---\ntags: ${tagsLiteral(tags)}\n---\n\n${body}\n`;
   126→
   127→  if (dryRun) {
   128→    console.log(`    [flow] ${date} → content/flows/${year}/${month}/${day}.md`);
   129→    if (tags.length > 0) console.log(`           tags: ${tags.join(', ')}`);
   130→    return true;
   131→  }
   132→
   133→  if (existing && !appendMode) {
   134→    console.log(`    ⚠  ${date}: flow already exists — skipped (use --append to add)`);
   135→    return false;
   136→  }
   137→
   138→  if (!fs.existsSync(outDir)) fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });
   139→
   140→  if (existing) {
   141→    fs.appendFileSync(existing, `\n\n---\n\n${body}\n`);
   142→    console.log(`    +  ${date}: appended → ${path.basename(existing)}`);
   143→  } else {
   144→    fs.writeFileSync(outPath, flowContent);
   145→    console.log(`    ✓  ${date}: content/flows/${year}/${month}/${day}.md`);
   146→  }
   147→
   148→  return true;
   149→}
   150→
   151→// ── Note processor ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   152→
   153→function processNote(filePath: string, filename: string): boolean {
   154→  const titleFromFile = path.basename(filename, path.extname(filename));
   155→  const slug = slugify(titleFromFile);
   156→
   157→  const outPath = path.join(NOTES_DIR, `${slug}.md`);
   158→  const altPath = path.join(NOTES_DIR, `${slug}.mdx`);
   159→  const existing = fs.existsSync(outPath) ? outPath : fs.existsSync(altPath) ? altPath : null;
   160→
   161→  const raw    = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
   162→  const stat   = fs.statSync(filePath);
   163→  const parsed = matter(raw);
   164→
   165→  const title   = (parsed.data.title as string | undefined) ?? titleFromFile;
   166→  const date    = (parsed.data.date as string | undefined)
   167→               ?? (parsed.data.created as string | undefined)
   168→               ?? formatDate(stat.mtime);
   169→  const existingTags: string[] = Array.isArray(parsed.data.tags) ? parsed.data.tags.map(String) : [];
   170→  const aliases: string[]      = Array.isArray(parsed.data.aliases) ? parsed.data.aliases.map(String) : [];
   171→
   172→  const { body, tags: inlineTags } = extractInlineTags(parsed.content);
   173→  const tags = [...new Set([...existingTags.map(t => t.toLowerCase()), ...inlineTags])].sort();
   174→
   175→  const escapedTitle = title.replace(/"/g, '\\"');
   176→  const noteContent = [
   177→    '---',
   178→    `title: "${escapedTitle}"`,
   179→    `date: "${date}"`,
   180→    `tags: ${tagsLiteral(tags)}`,
   181→    `aliases: ${tagsLiteral(aliases)}`,
   182→    '---',
   183→    '',
   184→    body,
   185→    '',
   186→  ].join('\n');
   187→
   188→  if (dryRun) {
   189→    console.log(`    [note] "${title}" → content/notes/${slug}.md`);
   190→    if (tags.length > 0) console.log(`           tags: ${tags.join(', ')}`);
   191→    return true;
   192→  }
   193→
   194→  if (existing) {
   195→    console.log(`    ⚠  "${slug}": note already exists — skipped`);
   196→    return false;
   197→  }
   198→
   199→  if (!fs.existsSync(NOTES_DIR)) fs.mkdirSync(NOTES_DIR, { recursive: true });
   200→  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, noteContent);
   201→  console.log(`    ✓  "${title}": content/notes/${slug}.md`);
   202→  return true;
   203→}
   204→
   205→// ── Dispatch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   206→
   207→function processFile(filePath: string): boolean {
   208→  const filename = path.basename(filePath);
   209→  const isFlow = DATE_FILE_RE.test(filename);
   210→  if (isFlow && notesOnly)  return false;
   211→  if (!isFlow && flowsOnly) return false;
   212→  return isFlow ? processFlow(filePath, filename) : processNote(filePath, filename);
   213→}
   214→
   215→// ── File discovery ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   216→
   217→function findPendingFiles(): string[] {
   218→  if (!fs.existsSync(OBSIDIAN_DIR)) {
   219→    console.error(`Directory not found: ${OBSIDIAN_DIR}`);
   220→    if (!vaultArg) {
   221→      console.error('Create it and place your Obsidian exports there:');
   222→      console.error('  mkdir -p imports/obsidian');
   223→      console.error('Or point directly at your vault: --vault /path/to/vault');
   224→    }
   225→    process.exit(1);
   226→  }
   227→  const imported = reimportAll ? new Set<string>() : loadImported();
   228→  return fs.readdirSync(OBSIDIAN_DIR)
   229→    .filter(name => !name.startsWith('.') && /\.mdx?$/.test(name))
   230→    .filter(name => !imported.has(name))
   231→    .sort()
   232→    .map(name => path.join(OBSIDIAN_DIR, name));
   233→}
   234→
   235→// ── Main ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   236→
   237→if (explicitFile) {
   238→  if (!fs.existsSync(explicitFile)) {
   239→    console.error(`Error: "${explicitFile}" not found.`);
   240→    process.exit(1);
   241→  }
   242→  console.log(`Processing: ${explicitFile}${dryRun ? ' (dry run)' : ''}`);
   243→  processFile(explicitFile);
   244→} else {
   245→  const pending = findPendingFiles();
   246→
   247→  if (pending.length === 0) {
   248→    const hint = reimportAll ? '' : ' (run with --all to re-import everything)';
   249→    console.log(`Nothing new to import in ${path.relative(process.cwd(), OBSIDIAN_DIR) || OBSIDIAN_DIR}${hint}.`);
   250→    process.exit(0);
   251→  }
   252→
   253→  const modeLabel = dryRun ? ' (dry run)' : reimportAll ? ' (--all)' : '';
   254→  console.log(`Found ${pending.length} file${pending.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} to import${modeLabel}:`);
   255→
   256→  let flows = 0, notes = 0, skipped = 0;
   257→
   258→  for (const filePath of pending) {
   259→    console.log(`\n  ${path.basename(filePath)}`);
   260→    const ok = processFile(filePath);
   261→    if (ok && !dryRun) {
   262→      markImported(path.basename(filePath));
   263→      DATE_FILE_RE.test(path.basename(filePath)) ? flows++ : notes++;
   264→    } else if (!ok) {
   265→      skipped++;
   266→    }
   267→  }
   268→
   269→  const parts: string[] = [];
   270→  if (flows   > 0) parts.push(`${flows} flow${flows === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
   271→  if (notes   > 0) parts.push(`${notes} note${notes === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
   272→  if (skipped > 0) parts.push(`${skipped} skipped`);
   273→
   274→  if (!dryRun) {
   275→    console.log(`\nDone: ${parts.join(', ') || 'nothing imported'}.`);
   276→    console.log(`Import history saved to ${path.relative(process.cwd(), IMPORTED_RECORD)}.`);
   277→  } else {
   278→    console.log(`\nDry run complete: ${parts.join(', ') || 'nothing to import'}.`);
   279→  }
   280→}
```

```json
  "new_string": "// ── File discovery ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// Recursively collect all .md/.mdx files, skipping hidden entries and .obsidian/.trash folders.\nfunction collectFiles(dir: string): string[] {\n  const results: string[] = [];\n  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir).sort()) {\n    if (name.startsWith('.')) continue;\n    const full = path.join(dir, name);\n    if (fs.statSync(full).isDirectory()) {\n      results.push(...collectFiles(full));\n    } else if (/\\.mdx?$/.test(name)) {\n      results.push(full);\n    }\n  }\n  return results;\n}\n\nfunction findPendingFiles(): string[] {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(OBSIDIAN_DIR)) {\n    console.error(`Directory not found: ${OBSIDIAN_DIR}`);\n    if (!vaultArg) {\n      console.error('Create it and place your Obsidian exports there:');\n      console.error('  mkdir -p imports/obsidian');\n      console.error('Or point directly at your vault: --vault /path/to/vault');\n    }\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  const imported = reimportAll ? new Set<string>() : loadImported();\n  return collectFiles(OBSIDIAN_DIR).filter(f => !imported.has(relKey(f)));\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. The key changes:

- **Recursive scan** — `collectFiles()` walks all subdirectories, so `journals/2024-01-15.md`, `Daily Notes/2024-01-15.md`, or a flat `2024-01-15.md` at the root all get picked up and routed to flows. Any non-date file anywhere in the vault goes to notes.
- **Skips `.obsidian/` and `.trash/`** — hidden directories (starting with `.`) are excluded automatically.
- **Relative path tracking** — `.imported` now stores `journals/2024-01-15.md` instead of just `2024-01-15.md`, so two files with the same name in different folders don't collide.
- **Log lines** show the vault-relative path (e.g. `journals/2024-01-15.md`) for clarity.

> AGENT

Done! Created commit **997d535** with message:

```
feat: scan Obsidian vault recursively with relative path tracking

Makes import-obsidian work with any folder structure:
- Recursively scans vault instead of flat root directory
- Supports journals in any subfolder (journals/, Daily/, etc.)
- Notes can be in any subfolder or at root
- Import history uses relative paths to prevent collisions
- Automatically skips hidden files and .obsidian/ folder
```

The untracked `.md` files in `content/flows/` and `content/notes/` are test imports — they're gitignored, so no harm there. Script is ready to use with your vault structure.

```json
  "old_string": "export function generateStaticParams() {\n  if (siteConfig.features?.flow?.enabled === false) return [{ slug: '_' }];\n  const notes = getAllNotes();\n  if (notes.length === 0) return [{ slug: '_' }];\n  return notes.map(note => ({ slug: note.slug }));\n}",
  "new_string": "export function generateStaticParams() {\n  if (siteConfig.features?.flow?.enabled === false) return [{ slug: '_' }];\n  const notes = getAllNotes();\n  if (notes.length === 0) return [{ slug: '_' }];\n  // Work around Next dev static-param checks for percent-encoded Unicode paths\n  // under `output: \"export\"` by including encoded variants only in development.\n  const slugs = new Set<string>();\n  for (const note of notes) {\n    slugs.add(note.slug);\n    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {\n      slugs.add(encodeURIComponent(note.slug));\n    }\n  }\n  return Array.from(slugs).map(slug => ({ slug }));\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

`noteUrl` already uses `note.slug` (canonical). The rest of the page references `note.slug` through `note.*` so it's clean. The fix is complete.

**Summary of changes to `src/app/notes/[slug]/page.tsx`:**

1. **`generateStaticParams`** — adds `encodeURIComponent(slug)` variants in dev mode so Next.js doesn't complain about unrecognised Unicode params
2. **`safeDecodeParam`** — new helper (same as posts page) that safely decodes percent-encoded slugs
3. **`generateMetadata`** — decodes the raw param before looking up the note
4. **`NotePage`** — decodes the raw param; all downstream calls (`getBacklinks`, `getAdjacentNotes`, `noteUrl`) use `note.slug` (the canonical value from disk) so encoding issues can't propagate further

```
     1→import { visit } from 'unist-util-visit';
     2→import type { Root, Text, Parent } from 'mdast';
     3→import type { SlugRegistryEntry } from './markdown';
     4→
     5→interface WikilinksOptions {
     6→  slugRegistry: Map<string, SlugRegistryEntry>;
     7→}
     8→
     9→export default function remarkWikilinks({ slugRegistry }: WikilinksOptions) {
    10→  return (tree: Root) => {
    11→    visit(tree, 'text', (node: Text, index: number | undefined, parent: Parent | undefined) => {
    12→      if (!parent || index === undefined) return;
    13→      if (!node.value.includes('[[')) return;
    14→
    15→      // Create fresh regex each time to avoid lastIndex issue with 'g' flag
    16→      const WIKILINK = /\[\[([^\]|]+?)(?:\|([^\]]+?))?\]\]/g;
    17→      if (!WIKILINK.test(node.value)) return;
    18→      WIKILINK.lastIndex = 0;
    19→
    20→      const newNodes: (Text | { type: 'html'; value: string })[] = [];
    21→      let last = 0;
    22→      let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
    23→
    24→      while ((match = WIKILINK.exec(node.value)) !== null) {
    25→        if (match.index > last) {
    26→          newNodes.push({ type: 'text', value: node.value.slice(last, match.index) });
    27→        }
    28→
    29→        const slug = match[1].trim();
    30→        const display = match[2]?.trim() || slug;
    31→        const entry = slugRegistry.get(slug);
    32→
    33→        if (entry) {
    34→          newNodes.push({
    35→            type: 'html',
    36→            value: `<a href="${entry.url}" class="wikilink wikilink--resolved wikilink--${entry.type}">${display}</a>`,
    37→          });
    38→        } else {
    39→          newNodes.push({
    40→            type: 'html',
    41→            value: `<span class="wikilink wikilink--broken" title="[[${slug}]] not found">${display}</span>`,
    42→          });
    43→        }
    44→
    45→        last = match.index + match[0].length;
    46→      }
    47→
    48→      if (last < node.value.length) {
    49→        newNodes.push({ type: 'text', value: node.value.slice(last) });
    50→      }
    51→
    52→      if (newNodes.length > 1 || (newNodes.length === 1 && newNodes[0].type !== 'text')) {
    53→        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
    54→        (parent.children as any[]).splice(index, 1, ...newNodes);
    55→        return index + newNodes.length; // skip inserted nodes
    56→      }
    57→    });
    58→  };
    59→}
```

> AGENT

Now I understand the full picture. Here's the analysis:

**The wikilink resolver** (`remark-wikilinks.ts:31`) looks up `[[Target]]` directly in `slugRegistry` by key. The registry is keyed by **slug** (e.g. `my-note-title`), not by original title. So `[[My Note Title]]` fails to resolve because the key `"My Note Title"` doesn't exist — only `"my-note-title"` does.

**Two complementary problems, two different fixes:**

**Problem 1 — Links in imported notes pointing outward:**  
`[[My Other Note]]` in an imported note won't resolve because the registry key is `my-other-note`.  
→ Fix: transform wikilinks during import: `[[My Other Note]]` → `[[my-other-note]]`, `[[Title|Label]]` → `[[my-title|Label]]` (display text preserved). Uses the same `slugify()` already in the script.

**Problem 2 — Links in other content pointing to an imported note:**  
Other notes/posts may contain `[[My Note Title]]` pointing at a note that becomes `my-note-title`.  
→ The `slugRegistry` already supports **aliases** — it registers each entry in `n.aliases` as an additional lookup key. So simply adding the original Obsidian filename as an alias (`aliases: ["My Note Title"]`) makes `[[My Note Title]]` resolve correctly from anywhere.

**My recommendation: do both.** They're independent and solve different directions of the same problem:

1. **In the import script**: slugify wikilink targets in body content + populate `aliases` with the original filename
2. **One-liner each**: reuse `slugify()` for links, add `titleFromFile` to aliases list

This way imported content is self-consistent, and cross-references from other content (posts, other notes) also resolve correctly without any content modification.

Shall I implement both?

> DEVELOPER

I think fix problem 1 seems enough, add aliases seems redundant. what do you think?

> AGENT

Agreed. Problem 2 only matters if existing Amytis content (posts, hand-authored notes) already has `[[Original Title]]` wikilinks pointing to notes being imported — which is an unlikely scenario. In the normal import flow, all cross-references are between Obsidian notes, and transforming wikilinks during import (Problem 1) covers that entirely. Aliases would be dead weight in most cases.

Let me implement it — a `transformWikilinks()` function using the existing `slugify()`, applied to the body after tag extraction:
