{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/amytis-claude-review/scripts/import-obsidian.ts",
  "old_string": "function tagsLiteral(tags: string[]): string {\n  return tags.length > 0 ? `[${tags.map(t => `\"${t}\"`).join(', ')}]` : '[]';\n}",
  "new_string": "function tagsLiteral(tags: string[]): string {\n  return tags.length > 0 ? `[${tags.map(t => `\"${t}\"`).join(', ')}]` : '[]';\n}\n\n// Transforms Obsidian [[Target Title]] → [[target-title]] and\n// [[Target Title|Label]] → [[target-title|Label]] using the same\n// slugify logic applied to filenames, so wikilinks resolve correctly\n// after import. Code spans/blocks are left untouched.\nfunction transformWikilinks(body: string): string {\n  const segments = body.split(/(```[\\s\\S]*?```|`[^`]+`)/g);\n  return segments.map((seg, i) => {\n    if (i % 2 !== 0) return seg;\n    return seg.replace(\n      /\\[\\[([^\\]|]+?)(?:\\|([^\\]]+?))?\\]\\]/g,\n      (_, target, label) => {\n        const slug = slugify(target.trim());\n        return label ? `[[${slug}|${label.trim()}]]` : `[[${slug}]]`;\n      },\n    );\n  }).join('');\n}"
}

Here's an assessment of what's worth testing, in priority order:

**1. `tests/unit/static-params.test.ts` — needs updating (high priority)**  
The posts route already has Unicode slug tests (lines 224–242) covering dev vs production behavior. The notes route fix is identical but has no such tests yet. Two tests should be added mirroring the post pattern:
- notes/[slug] includes raw + encoded Unicode slug in dev
- notes/[slug] includes only raw slug in production

**2. `src/lib/remark-wikilinks.ts` — new unit test (medium priority)**  
No test exists for this file at all. The new behavior (prefer `entry.title` over slug as display fallback) is a good candidate for a focused unit test with a mock registry — covering: resolved link shows title, explicit label wins over title, broken link falls back to slug.

**3. `scripts/import-obsidian.ts` — tooling test (medium priority)**  
Following the `tests/tooling/new-post.test.ts` pattern. The non-trivial logic — tag extraction, wikilink transformation, flow vs note routing, recursive scanning — would benefit from tests. The `spawnSync` approach works but is slow for this many behaviors; unit-testing the transform functions directly (like `extractInlineTags`, `transformWikilinks`) would be more effective.

**What doesn't need tests:**  
- The Flow/Note page changes (adding `Comments`, `ShareBar`) are pure composition with no logic  
- The lint fix (`if/else` instead of ternary) is trivial

Would you like me to implement all three, or start with the highest priority (static-params + remark-wikilinks)?

     1→import { describe, test, expect } from 'bun:test';
     2→import rehypeImageMetadata from './rehype-image-metadata';
     3→import type { Root, Element } from 'hast';
     4→
     5→/** Build a minimal HAST tree containing a single <img> node. */
     6→function makeTree(src: string): Root {
     7→  return {
     8→    type: 'root',
     9→    children: [
    10→      {
    11→        type: 'element',
    12→        tagName: 'img',
    13→        properties: { src },
    14→        children: [],
    15→      } satisfies Element,
    16→    ],
    17→  };
    18→}
    19→
    20→function imgSrc(tree: Root): unknown {
    21→  return (tree.children[0] as Element).properties.src;
    22→}
    23→
    24→describe('rehype-image-metadata', () => {
    25→  describe('relative paths (./)', () => {

    48→    });

  "content": "import { describe, test, expect } from 'bun:test';\nimport remarkWikilinks from './remark-wikilinks';\nimport type { Root, Paragraph } from 'mdast';\nimport type { SlugRegistryEntry } from './markdown';\n\nfunction makeTree(text: string): Root {\n  return {\n    type: 'root',\n    children: [{\n      type: 'paragraph',\n      children: [{ type: 'text', value: text }],\n    } as Paragraph],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction getChildren(tree: Root) {\n  return (tree.children[0] as Paragraph).children;\n}\n\nconst registry = new Map<string, SlugRegistryEntry>([\n  ['my-note',      { url: '/notes/my-note',           type: 'note', title: 'My Note' }],\n  ['another-post', { url: '/posts/another-post',      type: 'post', title: 'Another Post' }],\n]);\n\ndescribe('remarkWikilinks', () => {\n  test('resolved link uses registry title as display text', () => {\n    const tree = makeTree('[[my-note]]');\n    remarkWikilinks({ slugRegistry: registry })(tree);\n    const [node] = getChildren(tree);\n    expect(node.type).toBe('html');\n    expect((node as { type: string; value: string }).value).toContain('>My Note</a>');\n    expect((node as { type: string; value: string }).value).toContain('href=\"/notes/my-note\"');\n    expect((node as { type: string; value: string }).value).toContain('wikilink--resolved');\n    expect((node as { type: string; value: string }).value).toContain('wikilink--note');\n  });\n\n  test('explicit label overrides registry title', () => {\n    const tree = makeTree('[[my-note|Custom Label]]');\n    remarkWikilinks({ slugRegistry: registry })(tree);\n    const [node] = getChildren(tree);\n    expect((node as { type: string; value: string }).value).toContain('>Custom Label</a>');\n    expect((node as { type: string; value: string }).value).not.toContain('>My Note</a>');\n  });\n\n  test('broken link renders as span using slug as display text', () => {\n    const tree = makeTree('[[missing-slug]]');\n    remarkWikilinks({ slugRegistry: registry })(tree);\n    const [node] = getChildren(tree);\n    expect(node.type).toBe('html');\n    expect((node as { type: string; value: string }).value).toContain('wikilink--broken');\n    expect((node as { type: string; value: string }).value).toContain('>missing-slug<');\n  });\n\n  test('preserves surrounding text nodes', () => {\n    const tree = makeTree('Before [[my-note]] after.');\n    remarkWikilinks({ slugRegistry: registry })(tree);\n    const children = getChildren(tree);\n    expect(children[0]).toMatchObject({ type: 'text', value: 'Before ' });\n    expect(children[2]).toMatchObject({ type: 'text', value: ' after.' });\n  });\n\n  test('resolves multiple wikilinks in one text node', () => {\n    const tree = makeTree('[[my-note]] and [[another-post]]');\n    remarkWikilinks({ slugRegistry: registry })(tree);\n    const htmlNodes = getChildren(tree).filter(n => n.type === 'html');\n    expect(htmlNodes).toHaveLength(2);\n    expect((htmlNodes[0] as { type: string; value: string }).value).toContain('>My Note</a>');\n    expect((htmlNodes[1] as { type: string; value: string }).value).toContain('>Another Post</a>');\n  });\n\n  test('text without wikilinks is unchanged', () => {\n    const tree = makeTree('No links here.');\n    remarkWikilinks({ slugRegistry: registry })(tree);\n    const children = getChildren(tree);\n    expect(children).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(children[0]).toMatchObject({ type: 'text', value: 'No links here.' });\n  });\n\n  test('post type wikilink gets wikilink--post class', () => {\n    const tree = makeTree('[[another-post]]');\n    remarkWikilinks({ slugRegistry: registry })(tree);\n    const [node] = getChildren(tree);\n    expect((node as { type: string; value: string }).value).toContain('wikilink--post');\n    expect((node as { type: string; value: string }).value).toContain('>Another Post</a>');\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/amytis-claude-review/tests/tooling/import-obsidian.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, test, expect, beforeAll, afterAll } from 'bun:test';\nimport { spawnSync } from 'bun';\nimport fs from 'fs';\nimport path from 'path';\n\nconst SCRIPT_PATH = 'scripts/import-obsidian.ts';\n\n// Test vault lives under tests/tooling so it stays out of imports/ tracking\nconst VAULT_DIR = path.join('tests', 'tooling', '__temp_obsidian_vault');\n\n// Output paths — use year 2099 to avoid colliding with real content\nconst FLOW_PATH  = path.join('content', 'flows', '2099', '12', '31.md');\nconst FLOW_DIR   = path.join('content', 'flows', '2099');\nconst NOTE_PATH  = path.join('content', 'notes', 'my-important-note.md');\n\ndescribe('Tooling: import-obsidian', () => {\n  beforeAll(() => {\n    // Daily note inside a journals/ subfolder (to test recursive scan)\n    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(VAULT_DIR, 'journals'), { recursive: true });\n    fs.writeFileSync(\n      path.join(VAULT_DIR, 'journals', '2099-12-31.md'),\n      '---\\ntags: [existing-tag]\\n---\\n\\nHello #world.\\n\\nSee [[My Important Note]].\\n',\n    );\n\n    // Regular note at vault root with a wikilink that has an explicit label\n    fs.writeFileSync(\n      path.join(VAULT_DIR, 'My Important Note.md'),\n      'A note. See also [[Other Note|click here]].\\n',\n    );\n  });\n\n  afterAll(() => {\n    fs.rmSync(VAULT_DIR, { recursive: true, force: true });\n    if (fs.existsSync(FLOW_DIR)) fs.rmSync(FLOW_DIR, { recursive: true, force: true });\n    if (fs.existsSync(NOTE_PATH)) fs.unlinkSync(NOTE_PATH);\n  });\n\n  test('--dry-run previews both types without writing any files', () => {\n    const result = spawnSync(['bun', SCRIPT_PATH, '--vault', VAULT_DIR, '--dry-run']);\n    expect(result.exitCode).toBe(0);\n    const out = result.stdout.toString();\n    expect(out).toContain('[flow]');\n    expect(out).toContain('[note]');\n    expect(fs.existsSync(FLOW_PATH)).toBe(false);\n    expect(fs.existsSync(NOTE_PATH)).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  test('YYYY-MM-DD.md is imported as flow with extracted tags and transformed wikilinks', () => {\n    const result = spawnSync(['bun', SCRIPT_PATH, '--vault', VAULT_DIR, '--flows-only', '--all']);\n    expect(result.exitCode).toBe(0);\n    expect(fs.existsSync(FLOW_PATH)).toBe(true);\n\n    const content = fs.readFileSync(FLOW_PATH, 'utf-8');\n    // Both frontmatter tag and inline #tag appear in output\n    expect(content).toContain('\"existing-tag\"');\n    expect(content).toContain('\"world\"');\n    // Inline tag removed from body\n    expect(content).not.toMatch(/#world\\b/);\n    // Wikilink target slugified\n    expect(content).toContain('[[my-important-note]]');\n    expect(content).not.toContain('[[My Important Note]]');\n  });\n\n  test('regular .md is imported as note with slugified filename and title', () => {\n    const result = spawnSync(['bun', SCRIPT_PATH, '--vault', VAULT_DIR, '--notes-only', '--all']);\n    expect(result.exitCode).toBe(0);\n    expect(fs.existsSync(NOTE_PATH)).toBe(true);\n\n    const content = fs.readFileSync(NOTE_PATH, 'utf-8');\n    expect(content).toContain('title: \"My Important Note\"');\n    // Wikilink target slugified; explicit label preserved\n    expect(content).toContain('[[other-note|click here]]');\n  });\n\n  test('scans vault subdirectories recursively', () => {\n    // Flow came from journals/2099-12-31.md (a subfolder) — confirms recursive scan\n    expect(fs.existsSync(FLOW_PATH)).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  test('--all flag reimports already-imported files', () => {\n    // First import establishes history; second with --all should still succeed\n    spawnSync(['bun', SCRIPT_PATH, '--vault', VAULT_DIR, '--flows-only']);\n    const result = spawnSync(['bun', SCRIPT_PATH, '--vault', VAULT_DIR, '--flows-only', '--all']);\n    expect(result.exitCode).toBe(0);\n    expect(result.stdout.toString()).toContain('✓');\n  });\n\n  test('fails gracefully when vault directory does not exist', () => {\n    const result = spawnSync(['bun', SCRIPT_PATH, '--vault', '/nonexistent/vault/path']);\n    expect(result.exitCode).not.toBe(0);\n  });\n});\n"
}

> AGENT

All green. Here's a summary of the three test files added:

**`src/lib/remark-wikilinks.test.ts`** (7 tests) — unit tests following the `rehype-image-metadata.test.ts` pattern; builds minimal mdast trees directly, runs the plugin against a mock registry, and asserts on the output HTML nodes. Covers: title as display text, explicit label override, broken link fallback, surrounding text preservation, multiple wikilinks, link type class, no-op on plain text.

**`tests/unit/static-params.test.ts`** (2 new tests) — mirrors the existing post Unicode slug tests exactly. Added `mockedNotes` variable, wired it into the mock, and added `Comments` to the component stubs. Tests dev mode (raw + encoded) vs production (raw only) for Chinese character note slugs.

**`tests/tooling/import-obsidian.test.ts`** (6 tests) — spawnSync-based integration tests with fixture files in a temp vault. Uses year 2099 to avoid colliding with real content. Tests dry-run, flow routing with tag extraction and wikilink transform, note routing with slug + label preservation, recursive subdirectory scanning, and `--all` history bypass.

the same\n+// slugify logic applied to filenames, so wikilinks resolve correctly\n+// after import. return path.relative(OBSIDIAN_DIR, filePath);\n+}\n+\n+function loadImported(): Set<string> {\n+  if (!fs.existsSync(IMPORTED_RECORD)) return new Set();\n+  return new Set(\n+    fs.readFileSync(IMPORTED_RECORD, 'utf8').split('\\n').map(l => l.trim()).filter(Boolean),\n+  );\n+}\n+\n+function markImported(filePath: string): void {\n+  if (!fs.existsSync(DEFAULT_OBSIDIAN_DIR)) CodeRabbit left **2 inline comments** on `scripts/import-obsidian.ts` and **1 pre-merge check warning**:

---

**Inline comment 1 — `slugify()` can produce empty string** ⚠️ Minor  
`scripts/import-obsidian.ts` lines 78–83

If a filename contains only stripped characters (e.g. `🚀.md`), `slugify` returns `""`, creating invalid file paths. Suggested fix:
```diff
 function slugify(title: string): string {
-  return title
+  const slug = title
     .toLowerCase()
     .replace(/[^a-z0-9\u4e00-\u9fff]+/g, '-')
     .replace(/^-|-$/g, '');
+  return slug || 'untitled';
 }
```

---

**Inline comment 2 — Append separator `---` may confuse YAML parsers** ⚠️ Minor  
`scripts/import-obsidian.ts` lines 166–168

When appending to an existing flow, `\n\n---\n\n${body}` uses `---` which is the YAML frontmatter delimiter. Suggested fix: use a proper markdown thematic break instead:
```diff
-  fs.appendFileSync(existing, `\n\n---\n\n${body}\n`);
+  fs.appendFileSync(existing, `\n\n* * *\n\n${body}\n`);
```

---

**Pre-merge check — Docstring coverage** ❌ Warning  
36.36% coverage vs. 80% threshold. This is a CodeRabbit configuration setting — it's flagging missing JSDoc comments on the new script functions. It won't block the merge but is worth noting.

---

Both inline issues are valid. The `slugify` fallback is a straightforward defensive fix. The `---` separator is a real concern since flow files have frontmatter — `* * *` is semantically correct markdown for a horizontal rule. Want me to address both?
